--- How to read the rest ---
mean_still_bad_count            : LOWER is better.
mean_emg_band_power_after       : LOWER is better, but check eeg_band_retention.
mean_eeg_band_retention         : large drops vs C1 = likely over-cleaning
                                   (relevant for C3, which excludes ALL
                                   non-brain components, not just ocular).
mean_autoreject_reject_fraction : for C2 (no ICA at all), a HIGH value here is
                                   expected/consistent -- it means blink/muscle
                                   contamination is being caught downstream by
                                   epoch rejection instead of ICA, at the cost
                                   of discarding more trials outright.
